:::info[ESD > ISO]

- You can use [**ESD > ISO**](https://github.com/abbodi1406/WHD/raw/master/scripts/esd-decrypter-wimlib-67t.7z) to convert ESD files to ISO. You can then use that ISO to install Windows normally.

:::

:::info

- **Consumer** version contains Windows 10 Home, Home Single Language, Education, Pro, and their **N** editions.
- **Business** version contains Windows 10 Enterprise, Education, Pro, and their **N** editions.
- `Windows 10 2004 (19041.84)` ESD files have been removed from Microsoft servers. NTriver links have been added for those files.

:::
